Contributing weakness flagged, not blamed: the thesis itself disclosed thin volume (0.48x 20d avg) at entry, same confirmation weakness noted for TXG's B-grade.
  - **HOOD -> "unlucky".** Anticipatory-limit support-pullback, extended once already via a documented 09-10 time_stop_review that re-confirmed the setup (unchanged bullish EMA stack, no negative news at that time). Stop worked exactly as designed.
